## 1 Operational objectives

Right hand with a gun, left hand with the original filling and one from the left shoulder.**Original filling component**, insert and close lock. One shot at a time; no additional independent load after firing, without allowing the player to hold the explosive body in flight.

## 2 Player Operating Steps

| Phase | Player Actions | It has to be done. |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Taking of guns/shooting | Shoulder slots press RGrip, R when machine is ready 2 Fire! | Generate original player cartridges along the current gun; retain original blast and reward effects |
| Open | Left hand out of the sidekick, new LGrip grab-barrel loading unit, and open it on the physical track. | Start current round after original acceptance; only open for insertion at actual open end |
| Load Fill | Release facility, left to left shoulder new LGrip. | Set aside an original filling component matching this gun, without immediate withholding |
| Load | Keep holding it. Keep moving in along the physical entrance and axis. | Confirm q=1 Inventory submitted, firearms/handheld correctly handed over, second non-repeated |
| Close | Release and recapture the facility. Close it down to lock. | Shots fired but not locked; end of round after real lock |
| Again. | Effective new R 2 | The original body, a single lock and the explosive path were correct, without automatic reloading |

## 3. Borders and quantity

- Weap_Boomer/Weap_Boomer_base; capacity 1 Reserve ceiling 11 It's static reference, actually read.
- L 0/S 1 Inserted L 1/S 0;showing/Notify without second.2 No bullets are produced, no bombs are unloaded.
- Release the reservation before insertion; insert a missing/receiving gun and keep one round and unclosed, with a closed-lock to fire.
- A non-new load filled; no incentive to copy/lost original loads if public inspection capability allows for opening of the check; this is not a new game extended to take out live ammunition.
- S 0 Fills cannot be manufactured; open space remains closed and unattended.
- The close wall of the gun at the gunpoint follows the original legal collision starting point and cannot produce grenades from the other side of the wall.

## 4 Models, grabs and feedback

Use Locust_Boomshot.Mesh.Locust_Boomshot_Magazine (formerly MagazineMesh)0 Quoted explicitly.ammo 2 Not as a default handheld resource; the gun is shown in a physical phase of cross-repeated handheld by Magazine and handheld.

(a) The original Boomshot_Barrel has flat motion drawings that have not yet been fully demonstrated as composite tracks;**We can't close the chain first.**. The axis is closed, open and inserted by physical trajectory. Arms are fixed in length; insertion can be directed and not automatically loaded.

The complete master, hold and customs hand-handing must be seen in close proximity.

## 5 1. Must be accepted and accepted

1. Original pick-up gun/shoulder/real single-shot, original player bullet and explosion, not replaced by AI cartridge.
2. Opening unin place is not allowed; error components, reverses, side rubs cannot be submitted.
3. One set-off/insert/lock/repeal; actual count and model consistent at each stage.
4. The plug is not closed and the subsequent original fire is closed; no automatic filling shall be allowed.
5. The number of uncut hands, missing half-route, after-cutting guns were taken in the correct numbers.
6. Zero spare, live ammunition, repeated notification/completed, close wall generation, no copy and no wall-to-wall launch.

## 当前原模型入座制作候选与实时来源核对（2026-11-10 02:58，仅源码/离线）

本轮明确原返回路径：原Notify_AmmoReload只从角色拆下原M0并显示枪内嵌入Magazine，未把该静态组件安回枪内；原ejection另取Magazine socket并使用KActorSpawnable。故这条原返回路径没有可直接等待/复用的静态件seated矩阵，VR需制作匹配原模型的入座标定。现有ULElib只读表/原Engine与MP_WarMachine导出已按原inventory偏移/尺寸/SHA复核，所选KActorSpawnable组件链未见显式旋转覆盖，但native constructor未序列化值仍未知，未假定0。实际来源代码读取当前socket字段/getter，不依赖缺失tag。

## 1. 操作目标

右手持枪，左手打开原装填机构、从左肩取一个**原装填组件**、插入并关闭锁定。一次装一发；不增加射后另一套独立上膛，不让玩家手持飞行中的爆炸弹体。

## 2. 玩家操作步骤

| 阶段 | 玩家动作 | 必须结果 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 取枪／射击 | 肩槽点按RGrip，机械就绪时R2开火 | 沿当前枪口生成原玩家弹体；保留原爆炸和奖励效果 |
| 打开 | 空仓后左手离开副握，新按LGrip抓枪管装填机构，按实际运动轨迹打开 | 原版接受后启动本轮；到实际开放端点才开放插入 |
| 取装填件 | 释放机构，左手到左肩新按LGrip | 预留一个与本枪匹配的原装填组件，不立即扣备用 |
| 装入 | 持续抓握，沿实际入口和轴连续送入 | 确认q=1库存提交，枪上／手持件正确交接，不能重复装第二份 |
| 关闭 | 松手后重新抓机构，沿原轨迹关闭至锁定 | 有弹但未锁定仍禁射；真实锁定后结束本轮 |
| 再射 | 有效新R2 | 原弹体、一次扣弹和爆炸路径正确，未夹带原自动补弹 |

## 3. 边界和数量

- 原类 Weap_Boomer／Weap_Boomer_base；容量1、备用上限11是静态参考，实际读取。
- L0/S1插入后L1/S0；重复入座／Notify不加第二发。未关闭时R2不生成弹体、不扣弹。
- 插入前松手归还预留；插入后失追／收枪保留一发和未闭合状态，再取须闭合才能射。
- 已装一发不新取装填件；若公共检查能力允许打开检查，不能复制／丢失原装弹，不能创造奖励；此检查不是扩展为取出实弹的新玩法。
- S0不能制造装填件；开放空机构仍可关闭，无新弹则不授完美。
- 枪口近墙遵循原合法碰撞起点，不能从墙另一侧生成榴弹。

## 4. 模型、抓点和反馈

使用 Locust_Boomshot.Mesh.Locust_Boomshot_Magazine（原MagazineMesh0已明确引用）。ammo2不作为默认手持资源；枪上Magazine与手持件按实际阶段互斥显示。

原 Boomshot_Barrel 有平移动画，尚未证明完整复合轨迹；**不能先做成铰链折开**。按实际轨迹得到闭合、开放与插入轴。手臂长度固定；插入可有导向，不自动装弹。

依次提示装填机构、左肩／原装填件、入口、关闭机构；到位反馈只一次。完整主握、持件和关机构手型都需近看及侧视。

## 5. 必须验收

1. 原拾枪／肩取／真实单发，原玩家弹体与爆炸，不能用AI弹体替代。
2. 打开未到位不允许入座；错误组件、反向、侧擦不能提交。
3. 一发预留／插入／锁定／返射；每个阶段实际计数和模型一致。
4. 插后未闭合禁射，关闭后一次原射击；不得自动补满。
5. 插前松手、半行程失追、插后切枪再取，数量与机构正确。
6. 零备用、已有弹、重复通知／完成、近墙生成，均无复制和穿墙发射。

## 6. 实施入口与待核对

复用 weapon_boomshot_interaction.h、原Notify_AmmoGrab/Release/Reload、ProjectileFire及现有资产／库存后端。本枪会话自行认证实际参数、原枪管完整曲线、组件入座尺寸、角度与接触范围。完美期限已定4秒，不以原ReloadDuration=2.5秒或原完美窗口代替。
